# Unit asset inventory — 2026-09-22

Generation-time snapshot: 237 unit folders; 1942 image/GIF files in graphics/units and graphics/art. At that scan, 1743 were Git-tracked and 199 were not, including pre-existing local references. These are historical generation counts, not a live Git-status report. The owner subsequently selected the 163 new batch assets and two icon corrections for publication; see [the exact retention register](../docs/GIT_ASSET_POLICY.md) and [publication scope](asset_completion_2026-09-22/README.md#git-publication-scope). Comparison-sheet links remain local-only.

Confirmed generation scope: 74 working-roster entries plus 10 new land-unit entries. 84/84 scoped units have all seven standard assets and a generated-art source.

## Working roster and new units

Unit names open their folders. The seven standard assets are the game icon, transparent icon, native idle, DAT 4x idle, UltraSharp 4x idle, blue DAT 4x idle and DAT 4x attack GIF. The game icon is 256px; the upscaled image is the full-body sprite.

Generated art reuses existing unit HD images or the established FLUX family-art collection where available. Shared family files retain their original names and are identified in the source column; this inventory does not relabel them as newly approved tier-specific artwork. War Chariot modes share the same DAT visual sources and generated artwork.

Flemish Militia now uses the correct current DAT icon 354 for unit 1699. The previous incorrect icon pair is preserved in the working backup folder.
